_source line 5086 · 2026-05-22T12:51:22.526Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have the full picture:

**v2c (L28-all-position) actual results** in `steering_results_L28_all.json`:
- Config: `position: "all"`, `peak_layer: 28`, `probe_accuracy: 0.7757`
- Only 6 alphas tested: [-20, -10, -5, 5, 10, 20] (reduced from 10 — no ±1, ±2)
- All Fisher p-values far from significance:
  - alpha -20: steering=16, control=16, p=1.0, p→c=1, c→p=3
  - alpha -10: steering=9, control=11, p=0.817, p→c=1, c→p=1
  - alpha -5: steering=4, control=6, p=0.749, p→c=1, c→p=0
  - alpha +5: steering=4, control=1, p=0.370 (best p-value!), p→c=0, c→p=0
  - alpha +10: steering=7, control=5, p=0.769, p→c=0, c→p=0
  - alpha +20: steering=10, control=11, p=1.0, p→c=0, c→p=0

The v2c results confirm that all p-values are well above the significance threshold, with the best case at alpha +5 still nowhere near statistical significance. Across the alphas tested, directional flips between prompt and control are minimal and negligible. I'm also noticing a data integrity issue where the v2c results were saved to a separate file rather than overwriting the original, which explains why the baseline distribution matches v1 exactly.

There's some confusion about the file paths — the log message indicates results were saved to `steering_results.json`, but the actual v2c data is in `steering_results_L28_all.json`. Either the script was modified to save to a different location without updating the log message, or the Agent copied the file after saving. Regardless, the v2c data with position="all" is definitely in the `_L28_all.json` file and shows different results from v1. I'm also checking on the status of L13 and L18, which appear to still be running since there's no "Results saved" message yet.

Now I'm seeing that Qwen is in the baseline phase and GPU 0 is free since L28-all finished. I should verify the other pending experiments like Gemma probe training, the bootstrap run with n=2000, and the DE translate Llama job, plus double-check that L13 and L18 are writing to separate output files.
```

</details>

_source line 5113 · 2026-05-22T12:52:11.928Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Let me check the total conditions for the bootstrap logs to understand progress better. The "Done" count tells me how many parallel batches have completed. But to understand the total tasks, I need to know n_seeds and n_conditions.

For bootstrap n=2000:
- Llama fast: 30 "Done" entries. Looking at the log, it's currently on en_zh with 1394 tasks. The total per condition would be 2000 seeds × 32 layers = 64000? That seems too many. Wait, let me reconsider. The "tasks" in joblib are individual bootstrap iterations. 

So with n_seeds=2000, each task is one seed, meaning 2000 tasks per condition. The Qwen fast log shows zh_en with 1394 tasks completed out of 2000, and 41 "Done" entries, which suggests it's cycling through multiple conditions. The four conditions are en_en, zh_zh, en_zh, and zh_en, each running 2000 bootstrap iterations.

Llama fast (32 layers) has completed 30 conditions and is currently on en_zh with 1394/2000 tasks. Qwen fast has finished 41 conditions and is on zh_en with the same progress. Gemma probe training is running the en_en condition with 1088/2000 tasks across 42 layers.

For the v2c analysis with L28-all-position, all six alphas came back non-significant with p-values ranging from 0.370 to 1.0, with the best result at alpha +5 showing p=0.370.  All-position steering performed no better than last-token steering, and directional flips were evenly split at 3 each direction—essentially noise. The actual results are stored in `steering_results_L28_all.json` rather than the main results file, which still contains the older v1 data. There's a data integrity issue where the log message claims results were saved to the standard path, but the file was either renamed or the save path was changed without updating the corresponding log output.

The v2c variant used only 6 alphas instead of 10, which makes sense since the smaller values showed minimal effect in v1. The math checks out perfectly—1768 setting lines across 136 samples with 13 configurations each. Now I should report these v2c findings and continue tracking the other layer results while checking the registry for any updates.
```

启动诊断性变体轮（3配置并行）后再判定 Track 3  | steering_107 |\\n| D010 | 2026-05-22 | Steering (plan_107) 升为最高优先级；C3 需统计加固但不作为唯一卖点 | xnli_baseline_110, mvp_002 |\\n| D009 | 2026-05-22 | C3 confirmed conflict-specific：XNLI MLP 对照证明非线性 en | xnli_baseline_110, mvp_002 |\\n| D008 | 2026-05-22 | C2 reframe：不对称性非冲突特异性，改为\\\"冲突信号继承模型一般跨语言迁移偏置\\\" | xnli_baseline_110 |\\n| D007 | 2026-05-22 | Dream 审稿结果处理：新增 3 个 P0 实验（非冲突基线、bootstrap 升级、EN↔DE | dream_20260522T023801 |\\n| D006 | 2026-05-22 | COMET 0.80 阈值不直接适用 back-translation：提升 plan_105 至  | comet_qwen_v5, mvp_probe_v1 |\\n| D005 | 2026-05-21 | 双模型 MVP CONDITIONAL PASS：Llama EN→EN 77.6% + Qwen  | mvp_probe_llama_v1, mvp_probe_v1 |\\n| D004 | 2026-05-21 | Llama MVP CONDITIONAL PASS → EXTEND：EN→EN 77.6%, 跨 | mvp_probe_llama_v1 |\\n| D003 | 2026-05-21 | 补采样至 2000 条 + 取消强制交集，每个模型用各自 verified 数据集 | mvp_probe_llama_v1 |\\n\\n\\n\", \"lines\": 60}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

```text
- Still needs zh_zh, en_zh, zh_en after this

Looking at the elapsed times and task progression, the Llama process went from 1088 to 1394 tasks in about 19 minutes, so reaching 2000 would take roughly another 38 minutes total.   "attachment": {
```
```json
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
```

_source line 5194 · 2026-05-22T12:57:33.289Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Critical analysis of the ROADMAP update:

1. **DDL May 25 — 3 days remaining**: This is extremely tight. The project is now in EMNLP 2026 ARR sprint mode.

2. **D013**: DDL confirmed, Paper Agent launched in Full Draft mode. Writing and experiments running in parallel.

3. **Key changes from previous ROADMAP**:
   - Paper Agent activated
   - Qwen Steering L23 being restarted (responded to my report)
   - GPU status updated (cuda:0 now Qwen, v2c completed)
   
4. **Auditing DDL feasibility**: C1 is solid, C2 is reframed but workable, and C3 won't make it. The steering experiments are split—half the variants are showing negative results while the other half are still running. Gemma and the German language experiments are ongoing, and the bootstrap sampling with n=2000 is still processing. The real constraint is whether I'll have clear experimental results in time, since the Paper Agent can handle the writing itself in three days if the data comes through.

   b) **Contingency planning**: If L13 and L18 also come back negative—which seems likely given that v1 and v2c are already showing negative results—I'll need to pivot to Route B, which focuses on probing analysis and interpretation rather than novel findings. The question is whether Route B has enough substance for EMNLP: C1 plus the reframed C2, across three models and multiple language pairs with solid methodology, might be sufficient if I can make the analysis rigorous and insightful.

   c) **Evaluating the contribution**: C1 shows that linear probes can reliably detect cross-lingual knowledge conflicts in the residual stream across models (Llama at 77.1%, Qwen at 75.3%, Gemma pending). C2 demonstrates that asymmetry in cross-lingual transfer is a general phenomenon rather than specific to conflicts, which the XNLI control experiment supports. The negative results are still coming in.

   d) **Current progress and timeline**: L13/L18 should finish within the hour, Qwen steering is restarting and needs 2-3 hours, German translation is nearly done with less than an hour remaining, bootstrap resampling is partway through across conditions, and Gemma probing is about halfway complete.

   e) **GPU allocation**: All four GPUs are actively running different experiments with good utilization across the board.

   f) **Contingency planning**: If both L13 and L18 steer negative, I'd need to pivot to Route B, which sits in a risky zone for the main conference but could work for Findings with the three-model, three-language approach and solid methodology—though the narrative around null results becomes critical.

   The roadmap is now grounded in reality, the Qwen restart is underway, and everything should wrap up within a day or two, giving me time to write and refine before the deadline.

Now I need to verify the paper framing and decision logs to make sure both Route A and Route B narratives are prepared, since L13 and L18 results will determine which direction we take.
```

</details>

D001\\n- id: D003\\n  date: 2026-05-21 14:26 UTC\\n  summary: 补采样至 2000 条 + 取消强制交集，每个模型用各自 verified 数据集\\n  rationale: Llama verify 保留率仅 33%（266/800 train），低于 300 阈值。ConflictQA 有 ~10K 条，扩至\\n    2000 可保证两模型各自 ≥300 有效样本。取消交集策略因为强制交集会进一步损失样本量，且跨模型对比不要求相同样本——各自 verified 集上报告相同指标即可，交集作为补充分析。20%\\n    标签翻转率确认了 M001 verify 步骤的必要性。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp_probe_llama_v1\\n    role: triggered_decision\\n    result: verify 保留率 33%, 266 train / 70 test, 20% flip\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D001\\n  - D002\\n- id: D004\\n  date: 2026-05-21 19:17 UTC\\n  summary: Llama MVP CONDITIONAL PASS → EXTEND：EN→EN 77.6%, 跨语言迁移不对称（ZH→EN 71.3% vs\\n    EN→ZH 59.6%），等 Qwen 结果做最终 verdict\\n  rationale: Llama 4 方向探针结果显示冲突信号可线性检测（所有方向 p < 0.01），但 EN→EN 未达 80% PASS 阈值（77.6%）。跨语言迁移存在但高度不对称：ZH→EN\\n    强迁移（71.3%），EN→ZH 弱迁移（59.6%，刚好低于 60% point 阈值但 p=9.7e-4 仍显著）。CONDITIONAL PASS 按\\n    D002 规则进入 EXTEND 评估。等 Qwen 跨模型验证后做最终 MVP verdict。不对称迁移本身是有价值的科学发现。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp_probe_llama_v1\\n    role: primary\\n    result: EN→EN 77.6% L28, ZH→ZH 72.1% L13, EN→ZH 59.6% L5, ZH→EN 71.3% L15\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D002\\n- id: D005\\n  date: 2026-05-21 20:33 UTC\\n  summary: 双模型 MVP CONDITIONAL PASS：Llama EN→EN 77.6% + Qwen EN→EN 75.0%，跨语言迁移不对称且跨架构一致（ZH→EN\\n    >> EN→ZH），等 COMET 验证后进入 research 阶段\\n  rationale: 两个模型全 4 方向探针结果统计高度显著（permutation p < 0.001）。核心发现：(1) 冲突信号在两模型两语言中均可线性检测；(2)\\n    跨语言迁移存在方向性不对称（ZH→EN 71-73% vs EN→ZH 60-67%），两模型一致；(3) 不对称程度与架构相关——Qwen（多语言训练比例更高）EN→ZH\\n    67.3% 远强于 Llama 59.6%。EN→EN 均在 70-79% CONDITIONAL 区间，未达 80% PASS，但跨语言迁移的质量和不对称发现的科学价值足以支撑继续研究。COMET\\n    翻译质量验证是最后一个前置条件。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - 最后更新: 2026-05-22 | 目标: EMNLP 2026 via ARR (DDL 2026-05-25, 3天)\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\n多语言 LLM 中，跨语言知识冲突的检测信号可在残差流中被线性探针检测，且跨语言迁移呈现**方向性不对称**（ZH→EN >> EN→ZH ~7.2pp），该不对称性跨架构高度一致。**关键发现（plan_110 XNLI 基线）**：该不对称性并非冲突特有——非冲突语义任务 (XNLI) 展现几乎相同幅度的迁移不对称性（Qwen +6.72pp, Llama +5.97pp）。这说明冲突信号**编码在模型的通用跨语言表征空间中**，继承其方向性偏置，而非独立的专用机制。\\n\\n**注意**: CLEAR 的发现是 task-dependent 的——entity-centric factual conflicts 由 linguistic affinity 主导，reasoning-intensive tasks 由 resource abundance 主导。我们使用 ConflictQA（entity-centric），引用 CLEAR 时必须限定为 entity-centric 场景。\\n\\n## Narrative Structure\\n\\n**机制分析驱动**（非 \\\"发现新现象\\\"）：\\n\\n1. **Phenomenon**: CLEAR 发现在 entity-centric factual conflicts 中，linguistic affinity 决定跨语言冲突解决，但未提供机制解释\\n2. **Mechanistic question**: 冲突信号在模型内部如何编码？与一般跨语言语义表征的关系是什么？\\n3. **Our answer**: 冲突信号可线性检测且跨架构一致，但其跨语言迁移模式（方向不对称性）**与一般语义任务 (XNLI) 无异**——冲突检测 piggyback 在模型已有的跨语言对齐结构上，而非专用子空间。通过层结构分析和因果干预验证这一机制。\\n4. **Methodological contribution**: 严格的 confound exclusion pipeline (COMET ablation + token overlap) + 非冲突基线对照，为跨语言 probing 研究树立方法论标准\\n\\n**Secondary framing**: 跨语言 probing 中方向性偏置的普遍性——English-dominant 模型的固有属性，影响所有跨语言迁移研究的结论解读\\n\\n## Competing Hypotheses (pre-registered)\\n\\n**H1 — English-anchored universal detection**:\\n冲突检测信号本身是 language-independent 的（\\\"是否存在冲突\\\" 是语言无关判断），编码在 mid-layer 共享表征空间中。final-layer language transition 引入 similarity-dependent 干扰，影响 resolution 但不影响 detection。\\n- 预测: mid-layer conflict feature 跨语言 Jaccard >> late-layer Jaccard (paired permutation test p < 0.05)\\n- 预测: 英语探针跨语言迁移更强（英语作为训练数据主导语言，表征空间以英语为锚点）\\n\\n**H2 — Language-pair-specific encoding**:\\n冲突特征从 mid-layer 起即为 language-pair-specific 的，linguistic distance 直接编码在冲突表征中。\\n- 预测: mid-layer 和 late-layer Jaccard 无显著差异，或 mid < late\\n- 预测: 探针迁移准确率与语言对 typological distance 强相关\\n\\n**MVP + plan_110 证据倾向**:\\nZH→EN 迁移优势（~7pp）在冲突和 XNLI 任务中均存在且幅度一致，说明这是模型跨语言表征的固有属性（H1 部分支持：英语锚点存在，但非冲突特有）。冲突信号编码在通用跨语言空间中。非线性成分（MLP EN→ZH gains）是否冲突特有待 XNLI 上的 MLP 对照验证。\\n\\n## Core Claims\\n\\n### Claim 1: Cross-lingual conflict detectability\\n**Statement**: 跨语言知识冲突信号可在残差流中被线性探针检测，准确率显著高于随机基线。\\n**Evidence needed**:\\n- ≥2 model architectures 的 within-language probe acc > 70% (balanced) ✅ (Llama 77.1%, Qwen 75.3%)\\n- Permutation test p < 0.001 ✅ (all   "toolUseResult": "{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"path\\\": \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod42/projects/clkc_mech_interp/paper_framing.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# Paper Framing — clkc_mech_interp\\\\n\\\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-22 | 目标: EMNLP 2026 via ARR (DDL 2026-05-25, 3天)\\\\n\\\\n## Core Insight\\\\n\\\\n多语言 LLM 中，跨语言知识冲突的检测信号可在残差流中被线性探针检测，且跨语言迁移呈现**方向性不对称**（ZH→EN >> EN→ZH ~7.2pp），该不对称性跨架构高度一致。**关键发现（plan_110 XNLI 基线）**：该不对称性并非冲突特有——非冲突语义任务 (XNLI) 展现几乎相同幅度的迁移不对称性（Qwen +6.72pp, Llama +5.97pp）。这说明冲突信号**编码在模型的通用跨语言表征空间中**，继承其方向性偏置，而非独立的专用机制。\\\\n\\\\n**注意**: CLEAR 的发现是 task-dependent 的——entity-centric factual conflicts 由 linguistic affinity 主导，reasoning-intensive tasks 由 resource abundance 主导。我们使用 ConflictQA（entity-centric），引用 CLEAR 时必须限定为 entity-centric 场景。\\\\n\\\\n## Narrative Structure\\\\n\\\\n**机制分析驱动**（非 \\\\\\\"发现新现象\\\\\\\"）：\\\\n\\\\n1. **Phenomenon**: CLEAR 发现在 entity-centric factual conflicts 中，linguistic affinity 决定跨语言冲突解决，但未提供机制解释\\\\n2. **Mechanistic question**: 冲突信号在模型内部如何编码？与一般跨语言语义表征的关系是什么？\\\\n3. **Our answer**: 冲突信号可线性检测且跨架构一致，但其跨语言迁移模式（方向不对称性）**与一般语义任务 (XNLI) 无异**——冲突检测 piggyback 在模型已有的跨语言对齐结构上，而非专用子空间。通过层结构分析和因果干预验证这一机制。\\\\n4. **Methodological contribution**: 严格的 confound exclusion pipeline (COMET ablation + token overlap) + 非冲突基线对照，为跨语言 probing 研究树立方法论标准\\\\n\\\\n**Secondary framing**: 跨语言 probing 中方向性偏置的普遍性——English-dominant 模型的固有属性，影响所有跨语言迁移研究的结论解读\\\\n\\\\n## Competing Hypotheses (pre-registered)\\\\n\\\\n**H1 — English-anchored universal detection**:\\\\n冲突检测信号本身是 language-independent 的（\\\\\\\"是否存在冲突\\\\\\\" 是语言无关判断），编码在 mid-layer 共享表征空间中。final-layer language transition 引入 similarity-dependent 干扰，影响 resolution 但不影响 detection。\\\\n- 预测: mid-layer conflict feature 跨语言 Jaccard >> late-layer Jaccard (paired permutation test p < 0.05)\\\\n- 预测: 英语探针跨语言迁移更强（英语作为训练数据主导语言，表征空间以英语为锚点）\\\\n\\\\n**H2 — Language-pair-specific encoding**:\\\\n冲突特征从 mid-layer 起即为 language-pair-specific 的，linguistic distance 直接编码在冲突表征中。\\\\n- 预测: mid-layer 和 late-layer Jaccard 无显著差异，或 mid < late\\\\n- 预测: 探针迁移准确率与语言对 typological distance 强相关\\\\n\\\\n**MVP + plan_110 证据倾向**:\\\\nZH→EN 迁移优势（~7pp）在冲突和 XNLI 任务中均存在且幅度一致，说明这是模型跨语言表征的固有属性（H1 部分支持：英语锚点存在，但非冲突特有）。冲突信号编码在通用跨语言空间中。非线性成分（MLP EN→ZH gains）是否冲突特有待 XNLI 上的 MLP 对照验证。\\\\n\\\\n## Core Claims\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 1: Cross-lingual conflict detectability\\\\n**Statement**: 跨语言知识冲突信号可在残差流中被线性探针检测，准确率显著高于随机基线。\\\\n**Evidence needed**:\\\\n- ≥2 model architectures 的 within-language probe acc > 70% (balanced) ✅ (Llama 77.1%, Qwen 75.3%)\\\\n- Permutation test p < 0.001 ✅ (all directions)\\\\n- Cross-lingual transfer acc > chance (50%) with statistical significance ✅ (all directions 64-72%)\\\\n**Status**: MVP 已验证 ✅\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 2: Cross-lingual transfer asymmetry as general model property [REFRAMED per D008]\\\\n**Statement**: 跨语言探针迁移呈方向一致的不对称（ZH→EN > EN→ZH ~7pp），但 **XNLI 非冲突基线展现几乎相同幅度的不对称性**（Qwen +6.72pp, Llama +5.97pp），说明这是 English-dominant 模型的一般跨语言迁移属性，非冲突特有。冲突信号继承了模型的通用跨语言表征结构。\\\\n**Evidence**:\\\\n- 冲突不对称性: Llama +7.22pp CI[1.1,13.6], Qwen +7.18pp CI[-1.2,13.4] ✅\\\\n- XNLI 不对称性: Llama +5.97pp CI[0.92,11.33], Qwen +6.72pp CI[2.36,11.73] ✅\\\\n- 两者量级无显著差异，CI 大幅重叠 ✅\\\\n- Token overlap 排除 ✅ | COMET confound 排除 ✅\\\\n- ≥1 additional architecture 验证 ❌ (plan_102 Gemma 进行中)\\\\n**Status**: XNLI 基线已完成，C2 reframed ✅⚠️ (待第三模型确认)\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 3: Non-linear encoding trend [DEAD — D012 双模型 CI 含零]\\\\n**Statement**: ~~冲突信号包含非线性成分~~ → 降为 supporting observation，不作为独立 claim。\\\\n**Evidence**:\\\\n- 冲突 MLP Δ (EN→ZH): Qwen +3.84pp CI[-3.57,+11.31] 含零 ❌; Llama +5.21pp CI[-2.21,+12.87] 含零 ❌\\\\n- XNLI MLP Δ (EN→ZH): Qwen -2.0pp, Llama -0.5pp — 方向对比成立但不显著\\\\n- **双模型 bootstrap 均不显著**，方向一致（冲突正、XNLI 负）可作为 suggestive trend 在 discussion 中提及\\\\n**Status**: ❌ 不作为论文 claim。Discussion 中作为 observation 报告。\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 4: Layer-wise conflict signal structure\\\\n**Statement**: 冲突信号在 mid-to-upper layers 集中，峰值层在不同模型架构中占比一致（Llama ~88% depth, Qwen ~67% depth 有差异——需更多模型确认 pattern）。\\\\n**Evidence needed**:\\\\n- Layer-wise probe accuracy curves for ≥3 models ❌\\\\n- Layer-wise CKA/SVCCA 量化 EN vs ZH 表征相似度 ❌\\\\n- 与 \\\\\\\"Lost in Multilinguality\\\\\\\" 的 language transition 层对比 ❌\\\\n**Status**: 未验证 ❌\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 5: Cross-lingual SAE conflict features (Track 2)\\\\n**Statement**: SAE 特征空间中存在跨语言共享的冲突编码特征，可与 language-identity 特征解耦。\\\\n**Evidence needed**:\\\\n- Qwen-Scope SAE 特征分析 on Qwen3-8B-Base ❌\\\\n- LangFIR random-token filtering 控制 ❌\\\\n- Cross-lingual feature Jaccard 分析 ❌\\\\n**Status**: 未开始 ❌\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 6: Causal evidence via representation steering (Track 3) [v1 NEGATIVE — D011 diagnostic round]\\\\n**Statement**: 探针权重方向具有因果意义——沿此方向 steering 可改变模型冲突解决行为。\\\\n**Evidence needed**:\\\\n- Probe-weight steering 显著改变 p↔c flip rate (vs random control) ❌\\\\n- ≥1 layer/position 配置 Fisher p < 0.05 ❌\\\\n**v1 result (NEGATIVE)**: L28, last-token, Llama — 全10 alpha 不显著 (Fisher p∈[0.31,1.00])，steering≈control。\\\\n**D011 诊断变体轮**: L13(mid-layer) + L18(MLP peak) + L28-all-position 并行测试中。\\\\n- 全部失败 → steering dead，论文无因果证据（RC8 unresolved），需加强分析深度补偿\\\\n- 任一成功 → 扩展验证（更多 alpha + Qwen），C6 成立\\\\n**Status**: v1 NEGATIVE ❌, 诊断轮进行中 ⏳\\\\n\\\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\\\n\\\\n### RC1: Translation quality confound [RESOLVED ✅]\\\\n**Concern**: 中文翻译质量差可能导致虚假的冲突信号或迁移失败\\\\n**Result**: Plan_105 COMET tercile ablation 证明翻译质量非 confounder。Qwen test: Low COMET (mean 0.585) → 66.35% acc, High COMET (mean 0.816) → 60.71% acc, delta=-5.64pp (反向), Fisher p=0.695。冲突信号检测不依赖翻译质量。Back-translation COMET ≥0.80 阈值过严（两步翻译天然拉低），但 ablation 实证排除了翻译质量 confound。\\\\n**Remaining**: Llama test COMET 完成后重复此分析确认一致性。\\\\n\\\\n### RC2: Token overlap confound [RESOLVED ✅]\\\\n**Concern**: 跨语言迁移可能由 EN-ZH 共享 token（数字、专有名词、标点）驱动\\\\n**Result**: Per-sample Jaccard 仅 3-4%（type-level 10-15% 被膨胀）。Qwen 无相关 (p>0.4)，Llama 显著负相关 (r=-0.15, p=0.012, tercile Δ=-23.9pp)。高 overlap 反而降低准确率，方向与 RC2 相反。**共享 token 不驱动跨语言迁移，迁移发生在语义表示层。**\\\\n\\\\n### RC3: Model coverage insufficient [HIGH]\\\\n**Concern**: 只有 2 个模型，无法声称 \\\\\\\"架构无关\\\\\\\"\\\\n**Mitigation**: 加 ≥1 不同架构（Mistral/Gemma），验证 ~7.2pp 不对称性\\\\n**Experiment**: P0 — additional model verification\\\\n\\\\n### RC4: Dataset size and diversity [MEDIUM]\\\\n**Concern**: ConflictQA 覆盖面有限（~1000 samples/model），只有 EN-ZH 一个语言对\\\\n**Mitigation**: Phase 1 聚焦 EN-ZH 验证核心机制，Phase 2 扩展多语言（≥1 额外语言对）\\\\n**Experiment**: P0 — multilingual extension (EN↔JA or EN↔DE)\\\\n\\\\n### RC5: Linear probe expressiveness [LOW]\\\\n**Concern**: 线性探针可能遗漏复杂非线性信号\\\\n**Mitigation**: MLP 对比实验已完成 ✅，非线性成分已量化\\\\n**Experiment**: Done — MLP vs Linear comparison\\\\n\\\\n### RC6: SAE base/instruct mismatch [MEDIUM]\\\\n**Concern**: Qwen-Scope SAE 训练在 base model 上，用于 instruct model 分析可能失配\\\\n**Mitigation**: Track 2 用 base model 做 SAE 分析 + 与 Track 1 instruct probe 交叉验证\\\\n**Experiment**: Track 2 设计已考虑\\\\n\\\\n### RC7: Steering failure precedent [MEDIUM]\\\\n**Concern**: \\\\\\\"Interpretability without actionability\\\\\\\" 证明 SAE steering 可能完全无效\\\\n**Mitigation**: 知识冲突与临床分诊的 5 维差异（SpARE 已证明知识冲突场景 steering 有效）；Track 3 显式报告失败模式\\\\n**Experiment**: Track 3 包含 negative result framing\\\\n\\\\n### RC8: Causal claims without intervention [HIGH — steering v1 NEGATIVE, diagnostic round in progress]\\\\n**Concern**: 探针只证明信号可检测，不证明因果关系\\\\n**Current status**: Steering v1 (L28/last-token/Llama) NEGATIVE。D011 诊断变体轮（L13/L18/all-position）进行中。\\\\n**Mitigation if steering dead**: 论文降为 \\\\\\\"probing study with strong methodological controls\\\\\\\"，通过 XNLI 对照、token overlap 排除、COMET ablation 等方法论贡献补偿因果证据缺失。坦诚报告 steering negative 作为 limitation + future work。\\\\n**Experiment**: D011 steering variant round (plan_107 v2a/v2b/v2c)\\\\n\\\\n### RC9: Non-conflict semantic baseline [CRITICAL — Dream 三档一致]\\\\n**Concern**: X→EN > EN→X 不对称性可能是英语主导模型的一般跨语言迁移属性，非冲突特异性\\\\n**Mitigation**: 用相同 pipeline 探针非冲突语义任务（sentiment/NLI），验证不对称性是否消失\\\\n**Experiment**: P0 — plan_110 (非冲突语义基线)\\\\n**判断标准**: 非冲突任务的不对称性 < 2pp → conflict-specific confirmed；2-5pp → partially specific（报告为 \\\\\\\"general cross-lingual component exists but conflict amplifies asymmetry\\\\\\\"）；≥ 5pp → reframe as general cross-lingual property\\\\n\\\\n### RC10: Bootstrap n 不足 [MAJOR — Dream 三档一致]\\\\n**Concern**: n=100 bootstrap 低于 Efron & Tibshirani (1993) 标准 n≥1000，CI endpoints 波动大\\\\n**Mitigation**: 升级至 n≥2000\\\\n**Experiment**: P0 — plan_111\\\\n\\\\n### RC11: Typological diversity 不足 [MAJOR — Dream 三档一致]\\\\n**Concern**: 仅 CJK 语言对，无法支撑 \\\\\\\"universal\\\\\\\" 声明\\\\n**Mitigation**: 添加 Indo-European 语言对 EN↔DE\\\\n**Experiment**: P0 — plan_112\\\\n\\\\n## Paper Structure (Tentative)\\\\n\\\\n1. **Introduction**: CLEAR gap → mechanistic question → H1 vs H2 → three-track approach → key findings preview\\\\n2. **Related Work**: Monolingual conflict mechanisms → Cross-lingual processing → SAE steering (successes & failures) → Cross-lingual SAE features\\\\n3. **Method**: Data construction (ConflictQA + translation + verification pipeline) → Track 1 probing → Track 2 SAE analysis → Track 3 steering\\\\n4. **Experiments & Results**:\\\\n   - 4.1 Cross-lingual conflict detectability (C1)\\\\n   - 4.2 Asymmetric transfer analysis (C2) + token overlap controls\\\\n   - 4.3 Non-linear components (C3) + layer-wise structure (C4)\\\\n   - 4.4 SAE feature analysis (C5) [if Track 2 ready]\\\\n   - 4.5 Steering experiments (C6) [if Track 3 ready]\\\\n5. **Discussion**: H1 vs H2 resolution → mechanism implications → limitations → connection to CLEAR\\\\n6. **Conclusion**\\\\n\\\\n## Scope Control\\\\n\\\\n**投稿最低要求 (must-have for submission)**:\\\\n- Claims 1-3 fully supported (cross-lingual detectability + asymmetry + non-linearity)\\\\n- Claim 4 partially supported (layer-wise analysis on ≥2 models)\\\\n- ≥3 model architectures\\\\n- ≥2 language言对，含 ≥1 非 CJK 语言对 (EN-ZH + EN-DE/FR)\\\\n- **非冲突语义基线** (sentiment/NLI probe, plan_110) — 验证不对称性的 conflict-specificity\\\\n- Token overlap control\\\\n- COMET translation quality validation\\\\n- Bootstrap n≥2000 (plan_111)\\\\n\\\\n**理想状态 (nice-to-have)**:\\\\n- Track 2 SAE analysis (Claim 5)\\\\n- Track 3 steering (Claim 6)\\\\n- 5+ language pairs covering multiple typological families\\\\n- Full CKA/SVCCA layer analysis\\\\n\\\\n**不做 (out of scope)**:\\\\n- 10-language full CLEAR replication\\\\n- Custom SAE training\\\\n- MoE model analysis\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 9307, \\\"truncated\\\": false}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {}}\"}",

Qwen-Scope SAE 特征分析 on Qwen3-8B-Base ❌\\n- LangFIR random-token filtering 控制 ❌\\n- Cross-lingual feature Jaccard 分析 ❌\\n**Status**: 未开始 ❌\\n\\n### Claim 6: Causal evidence via representation steering (Track 3) [v1 NEGATIVE — D011 diagnostic round]\\n**Statement**: 探针权重方向具有因果意义——沿此方向 steering 可改变模型冲突解决行为。\\n**Evidence needed**:\\n- Probe-weight steering 显著改变 p↔c flip rate (vs random control) ❌\\n- ≥1 layer/position 配置 Fisher p < 0.05 ❌\\n**v1 result (NEGATIVE)**: L28, last-token, Llama — 全10 alpha 不显著 (Fisher p∈[0.31,1.00])，steering≈control。\\n**D011 诊断变体轮**: L13(mid-layer) + L18(MLP peak) + L28-all-position 并行测试中。\\n- 全部失败 → steering dead，论文无因果证据（RC8 unresolved），需加强分析深度补偿\\n- 任一成功 → 扩展验证（更多 alpha + Qwen），C6 成立\\n**Status**: v1 NEGATIVE ❌, 诊断轮进行中 ⏳\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n### RC1: Translation quality confound [RESOLVED ✅]\\n**Concern**: 中文翻译质量差可能导致虚假的冲突信号或迁移失败\\n**Result**: Plan_105 COMET tercile ablation 证明翻译质量非 confounder。Qwen test: Low COMET (mean 0.585) → 66.35% acc, High COMET (mean 0.816) → 60.71% acc, delta=-5.64pp (反向), Fisher p=0.695。冲突信号检测不依赖翻译质量。Back-translation COMET ≥0.80 阈值过严（两步翻译天然拉低），但 ablation 实证排除了翻译质量 confound。\\n**Remaining**: Llama test COMET 完成后重复此分析确认一致性。\\n\\n### RC2: Token overlap confound [RESOLVED ✅]\\n**Concern**: 跨语言迁移可能由 EN-ZH 共享 token（数字、专有名词、标点）驱动\\n**Result**: Per-sample Jaccard 仅 3-4%（type-level 10-15% 被膨胀）。Qwen 无相关 (p>0.4)，Llama 显著负相关 (r=-0.15, p=0.012, tercile Δ=-23.9pp)。高 overlap 反而降低准确率，方向与 RC2 相反。**共享 token 不驱动跨语言迁移，迁移发生在语义表示层。**\\n\\n### RC3: Model coverage insufficient [HIGH]\\n**Concern**: 只有 2 个模型，无法声称 \\\"架构无关\\\"\\n**Mitigation**: 加 ≥1 不同架构（Mistral/Gemma），验证 ~7.2pp 不对称性\\n**Experiment**: P0 — additional model verification\\n\\n### RC4: Dataset size and diversity [MEDIUM]\\n**Concern**: ConflictQA 覆盖面有限（~1000 samples/model），只有 EN-ZH 一个语言对\\n**Mitigation**: Phase 1 聚焦 EN-ZH 验证核心机制，Phase 2 扩展多语言（≥1 额外语言对）\\n**Experiment**: P0 — multilingual extension (EN↔JA or EN↔DE)\\n\\n### RC5: Linear probe expressiveness [LOW]\\n**Concern**: 线性探针可能遗漏复杂非线性信号\\n**Mitigation**: MLP 对比实验已完成 ✅，非线性成分已量化\\n**Experiment**: Done — MLP vs Linear comparison\\n\\n### RC6: SAE base/instruct mismatch [MEDIUM]\\n**Concern**: Qwen-Scope SAE 训练在 base model 上，用于 instruct model 分析可能失配\\n**Mitigation**: Track 2 用 base model 做 SAE 分析 + 与 Track 1 instruct probe 交叉验证\\n**Experiment**: Track 2 设计已考虑\\n\\n### RC7: Steering failure precedent [MEDIUM]\\n**Concern**: \\\"Interpretability without actionability\\\" 证明 SAE steering 可能完全无效\\n**Mitigation**: 知识冲突与临床分诊的 5 维差异（SpARE 已证明知识冲突场景 steering 有效）；Track 3 显式报告失败模式\\n**Experiment**: Track 3 包含 negative result framing\\n\\n### RC8: Causal claims without intervention [HIGH — steering v1 NEGATIVE, diagnostic round in progress]\\n**Concern**: 探针只证明信号可检测，不证明因果关系\\n**Current status**: Steering v1 (L28/last-token/Llama) NEGATIVE。D011 诊断变体轮（L13/L18/all-position）进行中。\\n**Mitigation if steering dead**: 论文降为 \\\"probing study with strong methodological controls\\\"，通过 XNLI 对照、token overlap 排除、COMET ablation 等方法论贡献补偿因果证据缺失。坦诚报告 steering negative 作为 limitation + future work。\\n**Experiment**: D011 steering variant round (plan_107 v2a/v2b/v2c)\\n\\n### RC9: Non-conflict semantic baseline [CRITICAL — Dream 三档一致]\\n**Concern**: X→EN > EN→X 不对称性可能是英语主导模型的一般跨语言迁移属性，非冲突特异性\\n**Mitigation**: 用相同 pipeline 探针非冲突语义任务（sentiment/NLI），验证不对称性是否消失\\n**Experiment**: P0 — plan_110 (非冲突语义基线)\\n**判断标准**: 非冲突任务的不对称性 < 2pp → conflict-specific confirmed；2-5pp → partially specific（报告为 \\\"general cross-lingual component exists but conflict amplifies asymmetry\\\"）；≥ 5pp → reframe as general cross-lingual property\\n\\n### RC10: Bootstrap n 不足 [MAJOR — Dream 三档一致]\\n**Concern**: n=100 bootstrap 低于 Efron & Tibshirani (1993) 标准 n≥1000，CI endpoints 波动大\\n**Mitigation**: 升级至 n≥2000\\n**Experiment**: P0 — plan_111\\n\\n### RC11: Typological diversity 不足 [MAJOR — Dream 三档一致]\\n**Concern**: 仅 CJK 语言对，无法支撑 \\\"universal\\\" 声明\\n**Mitigation**: 添加 Indo-European 语言对 EN↔DE\\n**Experiment**: P0 — plan_112\\n\\n## Paper Structure (Tentative)\\n\\n1. **Introduction**: CLEAR gap → mechanistic question → H1 vs H2 → three-track approach → key findings preview\\n2. **Related Work**: Monolingual conflict mechanisms → Cross-lingual processing → SAE steering (successes & failures) → Cross-lingual SAE
```text
Comprehensive analysis of paper_framing.md in light of DDL May 25 (3 days):

## Critical Issues to Flag

### 1. paper_framing.md Scope Control 过时
"投稿最低要求" 列出了:
- "Claims 1-3 fully supported (cross-lingual detectability + asymmetry + non-linearity)"
- But **C3 is DEAD** (D012). paper_framing still lists C3 as "must-have for submission" — this contradicts D012's decision.


- C4 remains unverified with no dedicated experiments planned, making it unlikely to materialize in the remaining time.
- Gemma probe training is underway and should finish soon, but probe accuracy is still unknown.
- German translation is progressing, though German probe training hasn't started yet.
- Bootstrap resampling is roughly 70% complete.

### 2. Paper Structure doesn't reflect D012 (C3 dead)
Section 4.3 "Non-linear components" needs to be removed or restructured since C3 is no longer a core claim.

### 3. Claim 6 (Steering) status
The steering experiments are mostly negative so far, with only a few variants still running. If they all fail, that entire section either becomes a negative results discussion or gets cut.

### 4. Refocusing the paper narrative
I'm realizing the actual story is much narrower than the original three-track framing. SAE work never started and steering is mostly failing, so really only the probing track is solid. The paper should center on detection working across models and asymmetry as a cross-lingual phenomenon, with the other approaches either removed or repositioned as exploratory work that didn't pan out.

### 5. EMNLP 2026 targeting
A Findings-track submission is realistic with the current results—strong detection across three models, cross-lingual asymmetry, and solid methodology. Main conference would require either deeper mechanistic insights or more surprising findings, which seems less likely given where things stand.

### 6. May 25 deadline feasibility
Checking what's actually needed versus what's ready to go.

C1 is done across Llama and Qwen, with Gemma probe training underway. C2's XNLI control is complete. The third model and language pair are still in progress—Gemma probes should finish within hours, but German translation and its probe training haven't started yet. Bootstrap resampling is about 70% done. Writing just began, and LaTeX formatting hasn't been touched.

The real bottleneck is clarifying the paper's framing before diving into writing—the current documentation still claims all three claims are fully supported, but C3 is essentially dead and needs to be removed from the narrative.

The paper structure section incorrectly frames C3 and C4 together, but C3 should be downgraded to an observation and C4 lacks verification. The three-track narrative (probing, SAE, steering) doesn't match reality—only the probing track has results, SAE never started, and steering is likely entirely negative. I should reframe this around mechanistic probing with rigorous confound control instead. C4's layer-wise structure claim also needs to be reassessed against what's actually been validated.

For the layer-wise probe accuracy curves, I can extract those from existing data without new experiments, but CKA and SVCCA have already shown negative results. The three-day deadline is feasible if Gemma and the bootstrap process finish within twelve hours and framing aligns quickly. The German language pair is nearly translated, but German probe training hasn't started yet—once translation finishes, I'll need to collect German activations and run probe training plus bootstrap, which adds significant time pressure.

The German activation collection could take six to twelve additional hours, and if GPU 0 gets occupied by the Qwen steering experiment, that work will have to wait for it to complete. I should prepare a comprehensive status report for the Director.
```

_source line 5300 · 2026-05-22T13:17:22.098Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Let me review this figure carefully as an independent reviewer.

**Figure 1: Pipeline Overview**

The figure shows three main blocks:
1. **Data Construction (§3.2)**: ConflictQA → Parametric Verification → Translation (Qwen3-8B, EN→ZH) → COMET Back-translation Validation → EN/ZH Parallel Conflict Data
2. **Probing Framework (§3.4-3.5)**: Residual Stream Extraction (last-token, all layers) → 4-Direction Transfer Protocol (EN→EN, EN→ZH, ZH→EN, ZH→ZH) → Bootstrap CI (n=2000)


3. **Confound Exclusion (§3.6)**: COMET Tercile Ablation ✓, Token Overlap Analysis ✓, XNLI Non-conflict Baseline ✓

Now I'm noticing a potential methodological issue: the same Qwen3-8B model that generated the translations is also the model being probed, which could introduce a confound where the model's own translation patterns influence the probing results rather than revealing genuine cross-lingual conflict representation. The translation model being the same as the probed model doesn't actually create a confound since the conflict signal stems from the underlying knowledge mismatch rather than translation artifacts. I'm also noticing the figure only shows the English-Chinese pair when German is apparently part of the paper, and it doesn't visually convey that this pipeline runs separately for each model even though the parametric verification step is per-model. The section references jump around a bit—it cites §3.2, §3.4-3.5, and §3.6 but skips §3.1 and §3.3, though that's probably fine for a figure.

The color scheme makes sense for the four-direction transfer protocol, using distinct colors for within-language transfers versus cross-language ones. Overall the pipeline accurately captures the methodology: starting with ConflictQA, running parametric verification per model, validating translations with COMET, executing the four-direction probing, computing bootstrap confidence intervals with 2000 samples, and applying three confound controls. Layer-wise analysis and steering experiments aren't depicted, which is reasonable if they're either detailed elsewhere or not central to the main findings.

A few minor gaps stand out—whether German should be included if it appears in the paper, making the multi-model aspect more visually prominent, and clarifying which specific models were used beyond Qwen3-8B for translation. The section references check out correctly against the methodology structure. These are small observations rather than critical flaws, so I'll note them but keep focus on the steering results that are coming up.
```

</details>

Looking at the statistical results, L13 shows dramatically different behavior than L28 — alpha=-20 and alpha=+5 both achieve highly significant p-values (0.000948 and 0.002005 respectively), confirming the mid-layer hypothesis was right. The pattern is striking: negative alphas show strong steering effects with control rates around 17-36%, while positive alphas show more modest effects, and there's an interesting reversal at alpha=+20 where control actually outperforms steering. I need to dig into why the directional flow differs across these alpha values to understand what's really driving these results. The directional breakdown at alpha=+5 shows all four flips going parametric→contextual, which aligns with positive alpha pushing toward contextual reasoning. At alpha=-20, the five contextual→parametric flips dominate, supporting the opposite effect. However, there's a troubling non-monotonic pattern at alpha=+20 where the control direction actually produces more flips than steering (28 vs 43, p=0.053), suggesting that extreme magnitudes might be degrading the model's coherence rather than systematically shifting its behavior.

```text
Now I'm weighing whether the L13 result is genuine or an artifact. The evidence supporting it includes the bidirectional effects at opposite alpha signs, consistent directional patterns, and the theoretical grounding in mid-layer processing. But there are real concerns: only 2 out of 10 alphas reach significance, which could be a multiple testing artifact; the non-monotonic reversal at alpha=+20 is suspicious; and the significant results appear asymmetrically at extreme and moderate alphas rather than showing a clean gradient.

However, applying Bonferroni correction to account for the 10 tests actually strengthens the case—both significant alphas pass the stringent 0.005 threshold, which is compelling evidence against random noise. Additionally, the probe accuracy at L13 reaches 76.1%, which is competitive with the original model's performance at L28, so the probe itself is reliable. This combination of multiple testing correction and probe validity suggests the L13 effect is likely real rather than spurious.

Now looking at the L18 layer results, which show the MLP peak with a probe accuracy of 73.9%. The data shows steering effects across different alpha values, with the strongest flips occurring at alpha=-20 (20.6% steering flips, 18.4% control flips) and diminishing effects as alpha approaches zero. Generating markdown table...

Layer 18 shows no significant steering effects across any magnitude tested—all p-values are well above significance threshold, meaning the steering direction is essentially equivalent to the control condition. Looking at the summary table, both model variants fail to show significant Fisher statistics at their peak layers, suggesting the causal steering approach isn't finding meaningful directional effects in these architectures.

But layer 13 in variant 2a is a complete game-changer—the p-value drops to 0.000948, which is highly significant. This mid-layer result validates the original hypothesis that the conflict signal lives deeper in the network than the final layers, and it means Route A is viable after all since there's genuine causal evidence for the probe direction at this intermediate depth.

The implications are striking: steering only works at layer 13, not at the upper layers, which suggests conflict detection happens specifically at the cross-lingual alignment layer—consistent with what Conneau et al. found about where cross-lingual representations converge. The effect is bidirectional too, with positive alphas driving p→c and negative alphas driving c→p, and we have two significant alphas that survive Bonferroni correction, so the statistical evidence is solid.

That said, there are real concerns I need to address: why does only layer 13 work when layers 18 and 28 don't? The alpha magnitudes required are quite large (alpha=-20 and alpha=+5), which aren't subtle perturbations, and at the extreme of alpha=+20 the effect breaks down nonmonotonically, suggesting the model's coherence collapses at those magnitudes.

Looking more carefully at the directional flips for alpha=+5: out of 27 total steering flips, only 4 are actual p→c directional flips—the rest are noise from changes to/from both or neither categories. This is a much smaller signal than the raw flip count suggests. I need to replicate this on Qwen at the equivalent mid-layer to confirm this isn't just an artifact of Llama's architecture. The directional consistency is actually the key insight here — positive alpha reliably pushes toward parametric→contextual while negative alpha does the opposite, even if the absolute numbers are modest. The real concern is whether this reflects genuine causal steering or just layer-level disruption that happens to have a directional bias. I should present both the statistical strength (significant Fisher test, Bonferroni-corrected) and the interpretive caveat (low ratio of directional to total flips suggests some noise or disruption effects).
```

**L13 有显著结果！** 这是 steering 的突破。详细审计两个变体的数据。

L13 关键数据点：
- alpha=-20: Fisher p=**0.000948**, steering 36.0% vs control 17.6%
- alpha=+5: Fisher p=**0.002005**, steering 19.9% vs control 6.6%
- 两个都通过 Bonferroni 校正 (0.05/10=0.005)

L18 全部 negative（最佳 p=0.447）。

```json
{
  "text": "[severity: critical] Steering v2 最终结果审计 — L13 突破性发现 + L18 negative\n\n## L13 (mid-layer, v2a): 两个 alpha 显著，通过 Bonferroni 校正\n\n| Alpha | Steering | Control | Fisher p | p→c | c→p |\n|-------|----------|---------|----------|-----|-----|\n| **-20** | **49 (36.0%)** | **24 (17.6%)** | **0.000948** | 1 | 5 |\n| -10 | 31 (22.8%) | 25 (18.4%) | 0.454 | 2 | 1 |\n| -5 | 22 (16.2%) | 17 (12.5%) | 0.489 | 2 | 1 |\n| -2 | 16 (11.8%) | 9 (6.6%) | 0.207 | 0 | 1 |\n| -1 | 7 (5.2%) | 6 (4.4%) | 1.000 | 0 | 0 |\n| +1 | 11 (8.1%) | 6 (4.4%) | 0.317 | 1 | 0 |\n| +2 | 16 (11.8%) | 7 (5.2%) | 0.079 | 3 | 0 |\n| **+5** | **27 (19.9%)** | **9 (6.6%)** | **0.002** | 4 | 0 |\n| +10 | 31 (22.8%) | 20 (14.7%) | 0.120 | 2 | 0 |\n| +20 | 28 (20.6%) | 43 (31.6%) | 0.053 | 2 | 2 |\n\n**积极发现**：\n1. alpha=-20 (p=0.00095) 和 alpha=+5 (p=0.002) **均通过 Bonferroni 校正** (阈值 0.005)\n2. 方向性一致：+alpha → p→c 增加（推向 contextual），-alpha → c→p 增加（推向 parametric）\n3. **层特异性**：L13 有效 vs L28/L18/L28-all 全 negative——mid-layer 是因果干预的关键位置\n4. 与 Conneau et al. (2020) mid-layer 跨语言对齐理论一致\n\n## 必须标记的注意事项 [warning]\n\n**1. 方向性翻转占比低**：\n- alpha=+5: 27 total flips 中只有 4 个 p→c（14.8%）\n- alpha=-20: 49 total flips 中只有 6 个 p→c+c→p（12.2%）\n- 大多数 flips 是非方向性的（both/neither 变化），可能反映 coherence 被干扰而非精确因果控制\n- **论文中必须诚实呈现**：steering 引起的是显著的行为扰动（Fisher 显著），其中包含方向一致的因果成分，但不是精确的 p↔c 开关\n\n**2. alpha=+20 反转信号**：\n- steering_flips=28 < control_flips=43, Fisher p=0.053\n- 极端 alpha 下 control 方向反而造成更多变化——可能意味着大幅度干预导致 coherence 崩溃\n\n**3. 仅 Llama L13 成功**：\n- 需要 Qwen 在对应 mid-layer 的复现（Qwen L23 正在运行）\n- 如果 Qwen 不复现，claim 范围必须限定为 \"Llama-specific\"\n\n## L18 (MLP peak, v2b): 全部 NEGATIVE\n\n所有 10 个 alpha 均不显著（最佳 p=0.447）。MLP peak layer 的探针方向不具因果性。这进一步说明 steering 效应具有层特异性——只在 mid-layer（跨语言对齐层）有效。\n\n## 对论文的影响\n\n**Route A 部分恢复**：L13 提供了因果证据，但强度有限（方向性比例低）。建议论文叙述：\n- \"L13 probe direction 引起的行为变化显著超过随机对照方向（Bonferroni-corrected p<0.005），方向与理论预测一致\"\n- \"效应具有层特异性，仅在 mid-layer（跨语言对齐区域）出现\"\n- 坦诚报告方向性翻转占比低，不 overclaim \"精确因果控制\"\n\n**C6 claim 建议措辞**：The probe direction at the mid-layer carries causal influence — perturbation along this direction produces significantly more behavioral change than random controls, with directional effects consistent with shifting conflict resolution. 不用 \"causally controls\" 这样过强的措辞。",
```

```text
1. **Qwen steering L23**: grep -c "Setting" returned 0 (exit code 1 means no matches found). But the log was showing "Baseline (84 samples)..." earlier, so the model was loading. It's on GPU 0 (14%, 16379 MiB). Either:
   - Still in baseline phase (generating without "Setting" messages)
   - Just finished loading and starting baseline

2. **GPU status**:
   - GPU 0: 14%, 16379 MiB — Qwen steering running ✓
   - GPU 1: 0%, 3 MiB — **EMPTY** (L13 completed)
   - GPU 2: 0%, 3 MiB — **EMPTY** (L18 completed)
   - GPU 3: 0%, 3 MiB — **EMPTY** (DE translate completed)

The DE translate job that was consuming 16203 MiB on GPU 3 has finished, leaving three GPUs idle while only GPU 0 continues with the Qwen steering task.

With the German translation datasets now complete, I can move forward with collecting DE activations using both Llama and Qwen models. The Qwen steering is currently running on L23 (its peak layer equivalent), but given that L13 proved most effective for Llama while L28 didn't, I'm wondering whether a mid-layer approach would work better for Qwen as well.

That said, this isn't really my decision to make — I'm here to review and report, not to plan the next steps. I should flag the GPU underutilization and note that the DE translation is finished, then let the Director decide what experiments to prioritize next.
```
